AND provider='gmail-mail'`).bind(state).first(); if(!r)return redirect('error','invalid_state'); await env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM calendar_oauth_states WHERE state=?').bind(state).run(); if(Date.parse(r.expires_at)Date.now()+90_000){return {account:r.account_email,token:await decryptCalendarSecret(r.access_token,env.CALENDAR_TOKEN_KEY)};} const refreshToken=await decryptCalendarSecret(r.refresh_token,env.CALENDAR_TOKEN_KEY); const response=await fetch('https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/x-www-form-urlencoded'}, body:new URLSearchParams({client_id:env.GOOGLE_CALENDAR_CLIENT_ID,client_secret:env.GOOGLE_CALENDAR_CLIENT_SECRET, grant_type:'refresh_token',refresh_token:refreshToken})}); const data=await response.json().catch(()=>({})); if(!response.ok||!data.access_token)throw appError('Gmailの認証期限が切れています。Gmailを再連携してください。',401); const token=await encryptCalendarSecret(data.access_token,env.CALENDAR_TOKEN_KEY); await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE portal_client_gmail_connections SET access_token=?,token_expires_at=?,updated_at=? WHERE user_email=?') .bind(token,Date.now()+Number(data.expires_in||3600)*1000-60000,new Date().toISOString(),session.user.email.toLowerCase()).run(); return {account:r.account_email,token:data.access_token}; } // Only inspect URLs already specified in official website/form columns. No arbitrary browsing or third-party scraping. function clientMailOfficialUrl(value){ try{ if(!value||String(value).length>1500)return ''; const u=new URL(String(value)); if(u.protocol!=='https:'||u.username||u.password||u.port)return ''; if(u.hostname==='localhost'||/\.(?:local|internal|localhost)$/i.test(u.hostname)||!u.hostname.includes('.'))return ''; if(/^(?:\d{1,3}\.){3}\d{1,3}$/.test(u.hostname)||u.hostname.includes(':'))return ''; if(['google.com','www.google.com','docs.google.com','facebook.com','x.com','instagram.com','wantedly.com'].includes(u.hostname.toLowerCase()))return ''; return u.toString(); }catch{return '';} } async function clientMailFetchOfficialEmails(url){ const controller=new AbortController(),timeout=setTimeout(()=>controller.abort(),4500); try{ const response=await fetch(url,{method:'GET',redirect:'manual',signal:controller.signal,headers:{'Accept':'text/html,text/plain'}}); if(!response.ok||!String(response.headers.get('content-type')||'').match(/(?:html|text\/plain)/i))return []; const size=Number(response.headers.get('content-length')||0);if(size>250000)return []; const reader=response.body?.getReader();if(!reader)return []; const chunks=[];let total=0; while(true){const {value,done}=await reader.read();if(done)break;total+=value.length;if(total>200000){await reader.cancel();return [];}chunks.push(value);} const bytes=new Uint8Array(total);let pos=0;for(const chunk of chunks){bytes.set(chunk,pos);pos+=chunk.length;} const html=new TextDecoder('utf-8',{fatal:false}).decode(bytes); const results=new Set(); const normalized=html.replace(/@|@|@/gi,'@').replace(/.|./gi,'.'); for(const match of normalized.matchAll(/(?:mailto:)?([A-Z0-9.!#$%&'*+/=?^_`{|}~-]+@[A-Z0-9.-]+\.[A-Z]{2,})/gi)){ const email=String(match[1]).replace(/[),;>'"\]]+$/g,''); if(CLIENT_MAIL_ADDRESS.test(email)&&!/(?:example\.com|wixpress\.com|sentry\.io|schema\.org|wordpress\.org)$/i.test(email))results.add(email.toLowerCase()); if(results.size>=12)break; } return [...results]; }catch{return [];}finally{clearTimeout(timeout);} } async function clientMailAddressSuggest(env,session,row){ const {record}=await clientMailCompany(env,session,row); const direct=CLIENT_MAIL_ADDRESS.test(record.email)?record.email:''; if(direct)return {email:direct,source:'スプレッドシートJ列',candidates:[direct]}; const sources=[record.url,record.website].map(clientMailOfficialUrl).filter(Boolean).slice(0,2); for(const src of sources){ const candidates=await clientMailFetchOfficialEmails(src); if(candidates.length){ const ranked=candidates.sort((a,b)=>{ const score=x=>/^(?:recruit|hr|jinji|contact|inquiry|info|saiyo)@/.test(x)?0:1; return score(a)-score(b); }); return {email:ranked[0],source:src,candidates:ranked,unverified:true}; } } return {email:'',source:'',candidates:[],message:'公式サイトに公開メールアドレスが見つかりません。手動で検索して確認してください。'}; } async function clientMailGetDraft(env,session,row){ const {record,canEdit}=await clientMailCompany(env,session,row); const draft=await clientMailStored(env,session,row); const match=!!draft&&draft.company_name===record.name; const template=clientMailFreshTemplate(match?draft:null,record.name,session.user.name); const gmail=await clientMailGmailStatus(env,session); const sent=await env.DB.prepare('SELECT status,provider_id,error FROM portal_client_email_send_jobs WHERE sheet_row=? AND company_name=?') .bind(row,record.name).first(); return {can_edit:canEdit,sender:gmail.account_email||'未連携（@dot-jp.or.jp のGmailが必要）',send_ready:gmail.connected&&gmail.configured, gmail,company:{row:record.row,name:record.name,recipient:CLIENT_MAIL_ADDRESS.test(record.email)?record.email:'', website:record.website||'',contact_url:record.url||'',emailed:false}, draft:{row,company:record.name,recipient:match?draft.recipient:(CLIENT_MAIL_ADDRESS.test(record.email)?record.email:''), cc:match?draft.cc:'',bcc:(match&&draft.bcc)?draft.bcc:CLIENT_MAIL_DEFAULT_BCC,subject:match?draft.subject:CLIENT_MAIL_SUBJECT, body_template:template,body:clientMailRender(template,record.name,session.user.name),verify_step:match?Number(draft.verify_step):0,saved:match},sent}; } function clientMailNormalizeBody(body,record,name,canEdit){ const text=String(body||'').replace(/\r\n/g,'\n').trim(); if(text.length<80||text.length>14000)throw appError('本文は80〜14000字で入力してください。',400); if(!text.startsWith(record.name+'\n採用ご担当者 様'))throw appError('本文先頭の会社名は選択した企業名と一致させてください。',400); const phrase='大学の'+name+'と申します。'; if(!text.includes(phrase))throw appError('大学名の後にはログイン者の名前を表示する必要があります。',400); if(!canEdit){ const canonical=clientMailRender(CLIENT_MAIL_TEMPLATE,record.name,name).trim(); const marker='〇〇'+phrase; const parts=canonical.split(marker); if(parts.length!==2)throw appError('メールひな形が不正です。',500); const suffix=phrase+parts[1]; if(!text.startsWith(parts[0])||!text.endsWith(suffix))throw appError('本文の編集は大学名だけ可能です。その他は代表またはクライアント部署員が編集できます。',403); const university=text.slice(parts[0].length,text.length-suffix.length); if(university.length<1||university.length>70||/[\r\n<>]/.test(university))throw appError('大学名の入力を確認してください。',400); } // Store replacement tokens, not hardcoded company / sender, so edits to rows do not create forged names. return text.replaceAll(record.name,'{{company}}').replaceAll(name,'{{name}}'); } async function clientMailSave(env,session,body){ const row=Number(body?.row),{record,canEdit}=await clientMailCompany(env,session,row,true); await clientMailTables(env.DB); const prev=await env.DB.prepare('SELECT status FROM portal_client_email_send_jobs WHERE sheet_row=?').bind(row).first(); if(prev)throw appError('この企業には送信処理履歴があります。再送を防ぐため保存できません。',409); const recipient=clientMailValidateAddress(body?.recipient,'宛先'); const cc=canEdit?clientMailAddressList(body?.cc,'CC').join(','):''; const bcc=canEdit?clientMailAddressList(body?.bcc,'BCC').join(','):clientMailAddressList(CLIENT_MAIL_DEFAULT_BCC,'BCC').join(','); const subject=canEdit?String(body?.subject||'').trim():CLIENT_MAIL_SUBJECT; const template=clientMailNormalizeBody(body?.body,record,session.user.name,canEdit); if(!subject||subject.length>250||/[\r\n]/.test(subject))throw appError('件名が不正です。',400); const now=new Date().toISOString(); await env.DB.prepare(`INSERT INTO portal_client_email_drafts (user_email,sheet_row,company_name,recipient,university,cc,bcc,subject,body_template,verify_step,updated_at,verified_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?,'',?,?,?,?,0,?,NULL) ON CONFLICT(user_email,sheet_row) DO UPDATE SET company_name=excluded.company_name,recipient=excluded.recipient, university='',cc=excluded.cc,bcc=excluded.bcc,subject=excluded.subject,body_template=excluded.body_template, verify_step=0,verified_at=NULL,updated_at=excluded.updated_at`) .bind(session.user.email.toLowerCase(),row,record.name,recipient,cc,bcc,subject,template,now).run(); return {saved:true,verify_step:0}; } async function clientMailSearchStart(env,session,body){ const row=Number(body?.row),{record}=await clientMailCompany(env,session,row); const draft=await clientMailStored(env,session,row); if(!draft||draft.company_name!==record.name||!draft.recipient)throw appError('先に保存してください。',409); if(Number(draft.verify_step)>=2)return {verify_step:2}; await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE portal_client_email_drafts SET verify_step=1,verified_at=NULL WHERE user_email=? AND sheet_row=?') .bind(session.user.email.toLowerCase(),row).run(); return {verify_step:1}; } async function clientMailVerify(env,session,body){ const row=Number(body?.row),draft=await clientMailStored(env,session,row); if(!draft||Number(draft.verify_step)!==1)throw appError('まずメールアドレス検索を開いてください。',409); await clientMailCompany(env,session,row); await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE portal_client_email_drafts SET verify_step=2,verified_at=? WHERE user_email=? AND sheet_row=?') .bind(new Date().toISOString(),session.user.email.toLowerCase(),row).run(); return {verify_step:2}; } async function clientMailPreview(env,session,body){ const row=Number(body?.row),{record}=await clientMailCompany(env,session,row,true); const draft=await clientMailStored(env,session,row); if(!draft||draft.company_name!==record.name||Number(draft.verify_step)!==2)throw appError('宛先を確認済みにして、変更内容を保存してください。',409); const canonical=clientMailRender(clientMailFreshTemplate(draft,record.name,session.user.name),record.name,session.user.name); if(canonical.includes('〇〇大学の'))throw appError('本文の〇〇大学を正式な大学名へ変更してください。',409); if(!clientMailValidateAddress(draft.recipient,'宛先'))throw appError('宛先が未入力です。',400); const sent=await env.DB.prepare('SELECT status FROM portal_client_email_send_jobs WHERE sheet_row=?').bind(row).first(); if(sent)throw appError('この企業は送信履歴があります。再送信できません。',409); const gmail=await clientMailGmailStatus(env,session); return {row,company:record.name,sender:gmail.account_email||'未連携',send_ready:gmail.connected&&gmail.configured, recipient:draft.recipient,cc:draft.cc,bcc:draft.bcc,subject:draft.subject,body:canonical}; } async function clientMailWriteO(env,row,company){ const {token,title}=await clientSheetTitleAndToken(env); const q=`'${title.replace(/'/g,"''")}'`; const range=`${q}!B${row}:O${row}`; const existing=await companySheetRequest(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${encodeURIComponent(COMPANY_SHEET_ID)}/values/${encodeURIComponent(range)}?valueRenderOption=UNFORMATTED_VALUE`,token); const cells=existing.values?.[0]||[]; if(String(cells[0]||'').trim()!==company)throw appError('シートの行が移動しました。別企業にチェックしないため停止しました。',409); if(cells[13]===true||String(cells[13]||'').toUpperCase()==='TRUE')return; const updated=`${q}!O${row}`; const write=await fetch(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${encodeURIComponent(COMPANY_SHEET_ID)}/values/${encodeURIComponent(updated)}?valueInputOption=RAW`, {method:'PUT',headers:{Authorization:`Bearer ${token}`,'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body:JSON.stringify({range:updated,majorDimension:'ROWS',values:[[true]]})}); if(!write.ok){const err=await write.json().catch(()=>({}));throw appError(`O列の更新に失敗しました（${write.status}）：${err.error?.message||'共有権限を確認してください'}`,502);} companySheetCache.expires=0; } function clientMailToBase64(bytes){ let s='';for(let i=0;i42){parts.push(part);part='';} part+=char; } if(part)parts.push(part); return parts.map(value=>'=?UTF-8?B?'+clientMailToBase64(encoder.encode(value))+'?=').join('\r\n '); } function clientMailMime(sender,data){ const cc=clientMailAddressList(data.cc,'CC'),bcc=clientMailAddressList(data.bcc,'BCC'); const lines=[`From: ${clientMailHeader('NPO法人ドットジェイピー')} <${sender}>`, `To: ${data.recipient}`,...(cc.length?[`Cc: ${cc.join(', ')}`]:[]),...(bcc.length?[`Bcc: ${bcc.join(', ')}`]:[]), `Subject: ${clientMailHeader(data.subject)}`,`Date: ${new Date().toUTCString()}`, 'MIME-Version: 1.0','Content-Type: text/plain; charset=UTF-8','Content-Transfer-Encoding: base64','',clientMailMimeText(data.body),'']; const utf8=new TextEncoder().encode(lines.join('\r\n')); return clientMailToBase64(utf8).replace(/\+/g,'-').replace(/\//g,'_').replace(/=+$/,''); } async function clientMailSend(env,session,body){ await clientMailTables(env.DB); const row=Number(body?.row); const prev=await env.DB.prepare('SELECT status FROM portal_client_email_send_jobs WHERE sheet_row=?').bind(row).first(); if(prev)throw appError(prev.status==='sent_unmarked'?'メール送信済みです。O列だけ再同期してください。':'送信履歴があります。二重送信防止のため送れません。',409); const data=await clientMailPreview(env,session,{row}); if(!data.send_ready)throw appError('先にGmailの送信権限を連携してください。',503); const {account,token}=await clientMailGmailToken(env,session); if(data.sender.toLowerCase()!==account.toLowerCase())throw appError('送信元のGmailアカウントが変更されました。再確認してください。',409); const providerKey=crypto.randomUUID(),now=new Date().toISOString(); const saved=await env.DB.prepare(`INSERT OR IGNORE INTO portal_client_email_send_jobs (sheet_row,company_name,user_email,recipient,provider_key,status,created_at,updated_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,'sending',?,?)`).bind(row,data.company,session.user.email.toLowerCase(),data.recipient,providerKey,now,now).run(); if(!Number(saved.meta?.changes||0))throw appError('同じ企業への送信処理が進んでいます。',409); let response,result; try{ response=await fetch('https://gmail.googleapis.com/gmail/v1/users/me/messages/send',{ method:'POST',headers:{Authorization:`Bearer ${token}`,'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body:JSON.stringify({raw:clientMailMime(account,data)})}); result=await response.json().catch(()=>({})); }catch(error){ await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE portal_client_email_send_jobs SET status=?,error=?,updated_at=? WHERE sheet_row=?') .bind('unknown','Gmail APIへの通信結果が不明です。Gmailの送信済みを確認してください。',new Date().toISOString(),row).run(); throw appError('Gmailの送信結果を確認できません。送信済みメールを調べるまで再送信しないでください。',502); } if(!response.ok||!result?.id){ await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE portal_client_email_send_jobs SET status=?,error=?,updated_at=? WHERE sheet_row=?') .bind('failed',`Gmail API ${response.status}: ${JSON.stringify(result?.error||{}).slice(0,280)}`,new Date().toISOString(),row).run(); throw appError(`Gmail APIが送信を受け付けませんでした（${response.status}）。Googleの送信権限を確認してください。`,502); } await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE portal_client_email_send_jobs SET status='sent_unmarked',provider_id=?,updated_at=? WHERE sheet_row=?`) .bind(String(result.id),new Date().toISOString(),row).run(); try{ await clientMailWriteO(env,row,data.company); await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE portal_client_email_send_jobs SET status='sent_marked',updated_at=?,error=NULL WHERE sheet_row=?`) .bind(new Date().toISOString(),row).run(); return {sent:true,sheet_marked:true,provider_id:String(result.id)}; }catch(error){ const msg=String(error?.message||error).slice(0,300); await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE portal_client_email_send_jobs SET error=?,updated_at=? WHERE sheet_row=?`) .bind(msg,new Date().toISOString(),row).run(); return {sent:true,sheet_marked:false,provider_id:String(result.id),warning:`Gmailは送信成功しましたがO列更新に失敗：${msg}。再送せずO列だけ再同期してください。`}; } } async function clientMailSyncSheet(env,session,body){ const row=Number(body?.row),job=await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM portal_client_email_send_jobs WHERE sheet_row=?').bind(row).first(); if(!job||job.status!=='sent_unmarked')throw appError('O列の再同期が必要なメールはありません。',409); if(job.user_email!==session.user.email.toLowerCase()&&!session.representative&&!session.developer)throw appError('送信者または管理者のみ再同期できます。',403); await clientMailWriteO(env,row,job.company_name); await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE portal_client_email_send_jobs SET status='sent_marked',updated_at=?,error=NULL WHERE sheet_row=?`) .bind(new Date().toISOString(),row).run(); return {sheet_marked:true}; }
